{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Vsf1pXhy1wMPYpUkCst183","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nThis is the environment for the ALFRED dataset.\\\\nauthor: Qineng Wang\\\\ndate: 2025-03-30\\\\n\\\\nModified to match verl-agent AlfWorld implementation:\\\\n- Dynamic mode switching between train/val\\\\n- Simplified reward: score * won\\\\n- Admissible actions included in observation\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport random\\\\nimport textworld\\\\nimport textworld.gym\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nfrom alfworld.agents.environment.alfred_tw_env import AlfredTWEnv, AlfredDemangler, AlfredInfos\\\\nfrom ragen.env.base import BaseLanguageBasedEnv\\\\nfrom ragen.env.alfworld.config import AlfredEnvConfig\\\\nfrom ragen.env.alfworld.utils import load_config\\\\n\\\\n_RAW_ENV_CACHE = {}\\\\n\\\\nclass AlfredTXTEnv(BaseLanguageBasedEnv):\\\\n\\\\n    # Mode mapping: RAGEN mode -> AlfWorld train_eval\\\\n    MODE_MAP = {\\\\n        'train': 'train',\\\\n        'val': None,  # Will use config.eval_dataset\\\\n        'test': None,  # Will use config.eval_dataset\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, config: AlfredEnvConfig = AlfredEnvConfig(), mode='train'):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Initialize AlfWorld environment.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            config: AlfredEnvConfig instance\\\\n            mode: 'train' or 'val' - determines which dataset split to use\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.config = config\\\\n        self.raw_env_config = load_config(self.config.config_file)\\\\n\\\\n        # Map RAGEN mode to AlfWorld train_eval\\\\n        self.current_mode = mode\\\\n        self._alfworld_mode = self._get_alfworld_mode(mode)\\\\n\\\\n        # Initialize raw environment\\\\n        self.raw_env = self._get_cached_raw_env(self._alfworld_mode)\\\\n        self.num_games = self.raw_env.num_games\\\\n        self.game_files = list(self.raw_env.game_files)\\\\n\\\\n        self.alfred_env = None\\\\n        self.current_game_file = None\\\\n        self.render_cache = None\\\\n        self.available_actions = None\\\\n        self.valid_action_count = 0\\\\n        self.render_mode = self.config.render_mode\\\\n        assert self.render_mode == 'text'\\\\n\\\\n    def _get_alfworld_mode(self, mode: str) -> str:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Convert RAGEN mode to AlfWorld train_eval string.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if mode == 'train':\\\\n            return 'train'\\\\n        else:\\\\n            # Use eval_dataset from config for val/test modes\\\\n            return self.config.eval_dataset\\\\n\\\\n    def _get_cached_raw_env(self, alfworld_mode: str) -> AlfredTWEnv:\\\\n        cache_key = (os.path.abspath(self.config.config_file), alfworld_mode)\\\\n        raw_env = _RAW_ENV_CACHE.get(cache_key)\\\\n        if raw_env is None:\\\\n            raw_env = AlfredTWEnv(config=self.raw_env_config, train_eval=alfworld_mode)\\\\n            _RAW_ENV_CACHE[cache_key] = raw_env\\\\n        return raw_env\\\\n    \\\\n    def _reinitialize_for_mode(self, mode: str):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Reinitialize the environment when mode changes.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        new_alfworld_mode = self._get_alfworld_mode(mode)\\\\n\\\\n        if new_alfworld_mode != self._alfworld_mode:\\\\n            # Close existing environment\\\\n            if hasattr(self, 'alfred_env') and self.alfred_env is not None:\\\\n                try:\\\\n                    self.alfred_env.close()\\\\n                except Exception:\\\\n                    pass\\\\n\\\\n            # Reinitialize with new mode\\\\n            self._alfworld_mode = new_alfworld_mode\\\\n            self.current_mode = mode\\\\n            self.raw_env = self._get_cached_raw_env(self._alfworld_mode)\\\\n            self.num_games = self.raw_env.num_games\\\\n            self.game_files = list(self.raw_env.game_files)\\\\n            self.alfred_env = None\\\\n\\\\n    def reset(self, seed=None, mode=None):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Reset the environment with a specific seed.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            seed: Random seed for game selection\\\\n            mode: 'train', 'val', or 'test' - if different from current mode,\\\\n                  reinitializes the environment with the new dataset split\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            observation: Initial observation text with admissible actions\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        try:\\\\n            # Handle mode switching dynamically\\\\n            if mode is not None and mode != self.current_mode:\\\\n                self._reinitialize_for_mode(mode)\\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 1726 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\nction_count = 0\\\\n            # Include admissible actions in render cache\\\\n            self.render_cache = self._format_observation(obs[0], self.available_actions)\\\\n            return self.render_cache\\\\n\\\\n        except (RuntimeError, RuntimeWarning) as e:\\\\n            print(f\\\\\\\"Error in reset: {e}\\\\\\\")\\\\n            next_seed = abs(hash(str(seed))) % (2 ** 32) if seed is not None else None\\\\n            return self.reset(next_seed, mode=self.current_mode)\\\\n\\\\n    def _format_observation(self, obs: str, admissible_actions: list) -> str:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Format observation to include admissible actions.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        actions_str = \\\\\\\", \\\\\\\".join(admissible_actions)\\\\n        return f\\\\\\\"{obs}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nAdmissible actions: [{actions_str}]\\\\\\\"\\\\n    \\\\n    def step(self, action: str):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Take a step in the environment using the provided action string.\\\\n\\\\n        Reward scheme (matching verl-agent):\\\\n        - reward = config.score * won (10.0 on success, 0.0 otherwise)\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            action: The action string to execute\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            observation: Updated observation with admissible actions\\\\n            reward: Sparse reward (score on success, 0 otherwise)\\\\n            done: Whether episode is finished\\\\n            info: Additional information dict\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        action_is_available = action in self.available_actions\\\\n\\\\n        # Execute action in environment\\\\n        obs, _, dones, infos = self.alfred_env.step([action])\\\\n        observation = obs[0]\\\\n        self.available_actions = infos[\\\\\\\"admissible_commands\\\\\\\"][0]\\\\n        done = dones[0]\\\\n        won = infos[\\\\\\\"won\\\\\\\"][0]\\\\n\\\\n        # Track valid actions for metrics\\\\n        if action_is_available:\\\\n            self.valid_action_count += 1\\\\n\\\\n        # Reward computation\\\\n        reward = self.config.score * float(won)\\\\n        if self.config.valid_action_bonus > 0:\\\\n            if self.config.success_gated_bonus:\\\\n                # Success-gated: bonus only awarded on successful episode completion\\\\n                if done and won:\\\\n                    reward += self.valid_action_count * self.config.valid_action_bonus\\\\n            else:\\\\n                # Unconditional: bonus per valid action step\\\\n                if action_is_available:\\\\n                    reward += self.config.valid_action_bonus\\\\n\\\\n        # Format observation with admissible actions\\\\n        self.render_cache = self._format_observation(observation, self.available_actions)\\\\n\\\\n        info = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"action_is_effective\\\\\\\": True,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"action_is_valid\\\\\\\": action_is_available,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\": won\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n        return self.render_cache, reward, done, info\\\\n    \\\\n    def render(self):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return current observation with admissible actions.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        return self.render_cache\\\\n\\\\n    def close(self):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Clean up environment resources.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self.render_cache = None\\\\n        if hasattr(self, 'alfred_env') and self.alfred_env is not None:\\\\n            try:\\\\n                self.alfred_env.close()\\\\n            except Exception:\\\\n                pass\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nif __name__ == \\\\\\\"__main__\\\\\\\":\\\\n    import os\\\\n    os.environ[\\\\\\\"ALFWORLD_DATA\\\\\\\"] = os.path.expanduser(\\\\\\\"~/.cache/alfworld\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    # Test basic environment functionality\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n=== Test 1: Basic environment with train mode ===\\\\\\\")\\\\n    env = AlfredTXTEnv()\\\\n    obs = env.reset(seed=42, mode='train')\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"Train observation (first 500 chars):\\\\\\\\n{obs[:500]}...\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    # Test mode switching\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n=== Test 2: Mode switching to val ===\\\\\\\")\\\\n    obs = env.reset(seed=42, mode='val')\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"Val observation (first 500 chars):\\\\\\\\n{obs[:500]}...\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    # Test step with admissible action\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n=== Test 3: Taking a step ===\\\\\\\")\\\\n    if env.available_actions:\\\\n        action = env.available_actions[0]\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"Taking action: {action}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"Reward: {reward}, Done: {done}, Info: {info}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"New observation (first 300 chars):\\\\\\\\n{obs[:300]}...\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    env.close()\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n=== All tests completed ===\\\\\\\")\\\\n    \\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.37}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"76290795-5d01-45f7-ac99-5018aad351eb","uuid":"01df0d9d-5e27-41b1-8499-d02af4cb2cc7","timestamp":"2026-05-14T16:50:42.279Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nThis is the environment for the ALFRED dataset.\\\\nauthor: Qineng Wang\\\\ndate: 2025-03-30\\\\n\\\\nModified to match verl-agent AlfWorld implementation:\\\\n- Dynamic mode switching between train/val\\\\n- Simplified reward: score * won\\\\n- Admissible actions included in observation\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport random\\\\nimport textworld\\\\nimport textworld.gym\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nfrom alfworld.agents.environment.alfred_tw_env import AlfredTWEnv, AlfredDemangler, AlfredInfos\\\\nfrom ragen.env.base import BaseLanguageBasedEnv\\\\nfrom ragen.env.alfworld.config import AlfredEnvConfig\\\\nfrom ragen.env.alfworld.utils import load_config\\\\n\\\\n_RAW_ENV_CACHE = {}\\\\n\\\\nclass AlfredTXTEnv(BaseLanguageBasedEnv):\\\\n\\\\n    # Test step with admissible action\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n=== Test 3: Taking a step ===\\\\\\\")\\\\n    if env.available_actions:\\\\n        action = env.available_actions[0]\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"Taking action: {action}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"Reward: {reward}, Done: {done}, Info: {info}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"New observation (first 300 chars):\\\\\\\\n{obs[:300]}...\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    env.close()\\\\n    print(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n=== All tests completed ===\\\\\\\")\\\\n    \\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.37}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nThis is the environment for the ALFRED dataset.\\nauthor: Qineng Wang\\ndate: 2025-03-30\\n\\nModified to match verl-agent AlfWorld implementation:\\n- Dynamic mode switching between train/val\\n- Simplified reward: score * won\\n- Admissible actions included in observation\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport os\\nimport random\\nimport textworld\\nimport textworld.gym\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom alfworld.agents.environment.alfred_tw_env import AlfredTWEnv, AlfredDemangler, AlfredInfos\\nfrom ragen.env.base import BaseLanguageBasedEnv\\nfrom ragen.env.alfworld.config import AlfredEnvConfig\\nfrom ragen.env.alfworld.utils import load_config\\n\\n_RAW_ENV_CACHE = {}\\n\\nclass AlfredTXTEnv(BaseLanguageBasedEnv):\\n\\n    # Mode mapping: RAGEN mode -> AlfWorld train_eval\\n    MODE_MAP = {\\n        'train': 'train',\\n        'val': None,  # Will use config.eval_dataset\\n        'test': None,  # Will use config.eval_dataset\\n    }\\n\\n    def __init__(self, config: AlfredEnvConfig = AlfredEnvConfig(), mode='train'):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Initialize AlfWorld environment.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            config: AlfredEnvConfig instance\\n            mode: 'train' or 'val' - determines which dataset split to use\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.config = config\\n        self.raw_env_config = load_config(self.config.config_file)\\n\\n        # Map RAGEN mode to AlfWorld train_eval\\n        self.current_mode = mode\\n        self._alfworld_mode = self._get_alfworld_mode(mode)\\n\\n        # Initialize raw environment\\n        self.raw_env = self._get_cached_raw_env(self._alfworld_mode)\\n        self.num_games = self.raw_env.num_games\\n        self.game_files = list(self.raw_env.game_files)\\n\\n        self.alfred_env = None\\n        self.current_game_file = None\\n        self.render_cache = None\\n        self.available_actions = None\\n        self.valid_action_count = 0\\n        self.render_mode = self.config.render_mode\\n        assert self.render_mode == 'text'\\n\\n    def _get_alfworld_mode(self, mode: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert RAGEN mode to AlfWorld train_eval string.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if mode == 'train':\\n            return 'train'\\n        else:\\n            # Use eval_dataset from config for val/test modes\\n            return self.config.eval_dataset\\n\\n    def _get_cached_raw_env(self, alfworld_mode: str) -> AlfredTWEnv:\\n        cache_key = (os.path.abspath(self.config.config_file), alfworld_mode)\\n        raw_env = _RAW_ENV_CACHE.get(cache_key)\\n        if raw_env is None:\\n            raw_env = AlfredTWEnv(config=self.raw_env_config, train_eval=alfworld_mode)\\n            _RAW_ENV_CACHE[cache_key] = raw_env\\n        return raw_env\\n    \\n    def _reinitialize_for_mode(self, mode: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Reinitialize the environment when mode changes.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        new_alfworld_mode = self._get_alfworld_mode(mode)\\n\\n        if new_alfworld_mode != self._alfworld_mode:\\n            # Close existing environment\\n            if hasattr(self, 'alfred_env') and self.alfred_env is not None:\\n                try:\\n                    self.alfred_env.close()\\n                except Exception:\\n                    pass\\n\\n            # Reinitialize with new mode\\n            self._alfworld_mode = new_alfworld_mode\\n            self.current_mode = mode\\n            self.raw_env = self._get_cached_raw_env(self._alfworld_mode)\\n            self.num_games = self.raw_env.num_games\\n            self.game_files = list(self.raw_env.game_files)\\n            self.alfred_env = None\\n\\n    def reset(self, seed=None, mode=None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Reset the environment with a specific seed.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            seed: Random seed for game selection\\n            mode: 'train', 'val', or 'test' - if different from current mode,\\n                  reinitializes the environment with the new dataset split\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            observation: Initial observation text with admissible actions\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        try:\\n            # Handle mode switching dynamically\\n            if mode is not None and mode != self.current_mode:\\n                self._reinitialize_for_mode(mode)\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 1726 字符] ...\\n\\nction_count = 0\\n            # Include admissible actions in render cache\\n            self.render_cache = self._format_observation(obs[0], self.available_actions)\\n            return self.render_cache\\n\\n        except (RuntimeError, RuntimeWarning) as e:\\n            print(f\\\"Error in reset: {e}\\\")\\n            next_seed = abs(hash(str(seed))) % (2 ** 32) if seed is not None else None\\n            return self.reset(next_seed, mode=self.current_mode)\\n\\n    def _format_observation(self, obs: str, admissible_actions: list) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Format observation to include admissible actions.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        actions_str = \\\", \\\".join(admissible_actions)\\n        return f\\\"{obs}\\\\n\\\\nAdmissible actions: [{actions_str}]\\\"\\n    \\n    def step(self, action: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Take a step in the environment using the provided action string.\\n\\n        Reward scheme (matching verl-agent):\\n        - reward = config.score * won (10.0 on success, 0.0 otherwise)\\n\\n        Args:\\n            action: The action string to execute\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            observation: Updated observation with admissible actions\\n            reward: Sparse reward (score on success, 0 otherwise)\\n            done: Whether episode is finished\\n            info: Additional information dict\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        action_is_available = action in self.available_actions\\n\\n        # Execute action in environment\\n        obs, _, dones, infos = self.alfred_env.step([action])\\n        observation = obs[0]\\n        self.available_actions = infos[\\\"admissible_commands\\\"][0]\\n        done = dones[0]\\n        won = infos[\\\"won\\\"][0]\\n\\n        # Track valid actions for metrics\\n        if action_is_available:\\n            self.valid_action_count += 1\\n\\n        # Reward computation\\n        reward = self.config.score * float(won)\\n        if self.config.valid_action_bonus > 0:\\n            if self.config.success_gated_bonus:\\n                # Success-gated: bonus only awarded on successful episode completion\\n                if done and won:\\n                    reward += self.valid_action_count * self.config.valid_action_bonus\\n            else:\\n                # Unconditional: bonus per valid action step\\n                if action_is_available:\\n                    reward += self.config.valid_action_bonus\\n\\n        # Format observation with admissible actions\\n        self.render_cache = self._format_observation(observation, self.available_actions)\\n\\n        info = {\\n            \\\"action_is_effective\\\": True,\\n            \\\"action_is_valid\\\": action_is_available,\\n            \\\"success\\\": won\\n        }\\n\\n        return self.render_cache, reward, done, info\\n    \\n    def render(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Return current observation with admissible actions.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return self.render_cache\\n\\n    def close(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Clean up environment resources.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.render_cache = None\\n        if hasattr(self, 'alfred_env') and self.alfred_env is not None:\\n            try:\\n                self.alfred_env.close()\\n            except Exception:\\n                pass\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    import os\\n    os.environ[\\\"ALFWORLD_DATA\\\"] = os.path.expanduser(\\\"~/.cache/alfworld\\\")\\n\\n    # Test basic environment functionality\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== Test 1: Basic environment with train mode ===\\\")\\n    env = AlfredTXTEnv()\\n    obs = env.reset(seed=42, mode='train')\\n    print(f\\\"Train observation (first 500 chars):\\\\n{obs[:500]}...\\\")\\n\\n    # Test mode switching\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== Test 2: Mode switching to val ===\\\")\\n    obs = env.reset(seed=42, mode='val')\\n    print(f\\\"Val observation (first 500 chars):\\\\n{obs[:500]}...\\\")\\n\\n    # Test step with admissible action\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== Test 3: Taking a step ===\\\")\\n    if env.available_actions:\\n        action = env.available_actions[0]\\n        print(f\\\"Taking action: {action}\\\")\\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\\n        print(f\\\"Reward: {reward}, Done: {done}, Info: {info}\\\")\\n        print(f\\\"New observation (first 300 chars):\\\\n{obs[:300]}...\\\")\\n\\n    env.close()\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== All tests completed ===\\\")\\n    \\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.37}}"}}}

structural\\\\nconversion.\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport re\\\\nimport random\\\\nfrom collections import \\\\\\\"look\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"inventory\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"examine \\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"clean \\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"heat \\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"cool \\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"move \\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"slice re.DOTALL)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef is_valid_action(action: str) -> bool:\\\\n    a = action.strip().lower()\\\\n    return any(a.startswith(p) or a == p.strip() for p in VALID_ACTION_PREFIXES)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef validate_example(ex, min_turns: int, max_turns: int):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return (ok, reason, n_assistant_turns, actions).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if not isinstance(ex, dict) or \\\\\\\"messages\\\\\\\" not in ex:\\\\n        return False, \\\\\\\"no_messages\\\\\\\", 0, []\\\\n    msgs = ex[\\\\\\\"messages\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    if not msgs or msgs[0][\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\"] != \\\\\\\"system\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return False, \\\\\\\"no_system\\\\\\\", 0, []\\\\n    if len(msgs) < 3 or msgs[1][\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\"] != \\\\\\\"user\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return False, \\\\\\\"no_user_task\\\\\\\", 0, []\\\\n\\\\n    actions = []\\\\n    assistant_turns = 0\\\\n    for m in msgs[2:]:\\\\n        role = m.get(\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\")\\\\n        content = m.get(\\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\")\\\\n        if role == \\\\\\\"assistant\\\\\\\":\\\\n            assistant_turns += 1\\\\n            ans = ANSWER_RE.search(content)\\\\n            if not ans:\\\\n                return False, \\\\\\\"missing_answer_tag\\\\\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\\\n            if not THINK_RE.search(content):\\\\n                return False, \\\\\\\"missing_think_tag\\\\\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\\\n            action = ans.group(1).strip()\\\\n            if \\\\\\\"user\\\\\\\":\\\\n            continue\\\\n        else:\\\\n            return False, f\\\\\\\"bad_role:{role}\\\\\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\\\n\\\\n    if assistant_turns < min_turns:\\\\n        return False, \\\\\\\"too_short\\\\\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\\\n    if assistant_turns > max_turns:\\\\n        return False, \\\\\\\"too_long\\\\\\\", assistant_turns, argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport random\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nVALID_ACTION_PREFIXES = (\\n    \\\"go to \\\", \\\"open \\\", \\\"close \\\", \\\"take \\\", action.strip().lower()\\n    return any(a.startswith(p) or a == p.strip() for p in VALID_ACTION_PREFIXES)\\n\\n\\ndef validate_example(ex, min_turns: int, max_turns: int):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return (ok, reason, n_assistant_turns, actions).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not isinstance(ex, dict) or \\\"messages\\\" not in ex:\\n        return False, \\\"no_messages\\\", 0, []\\n    msgs =       return False, \\\"no_user_task\\\", 0, []\\n\\n    actions = []\\n    assistant_turns = 0\\n    for m in msgs[2:]:\\n        role = m.get(\\\"role\\\")\\n        content = m.get(\\\"content\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n        if role == \\\"assistant\\\":\\n            assistant_turns += 1\\n            ans = ANSWER_RE.search(content)\\n            if not ans:\\n                return False, \\\"missing_answer_tag\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\n            if not THINK_RE.search(content):\\n                return False, \\\"missing_think_tag\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\n            action = ans.group(1).strip()\\n            if not is_valid_action(action):\\n                return False, \\\"invalid_action\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\n            actions.append(action)\\n return False, \\\"too_short\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\n    if assistant_turns > max_turns:\\n        return False, \\\"too_long\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\n    return True, \\\"ok\\\", assistant_turns, actions\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        \\\"--input\\\",\\n        default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/expert_trajectories/expert_trajectories.jsonl\\\",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        \\\"--output-dir\\\",\\n        default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/expert_sft/\\\",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--min-turns\\\", type=int, default=2)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--max-turns\\\", type=int, default=40)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--val-frac\\\", type=float, default=0.05,\\n                        help=\\\"Fraction held out for val (0 = no split).\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--dry-run\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\",\\n                        help=\\\"Only print stats, do not write output.\\\")\\n    args = turn_counts = []\\n    action_first = Counter()\\n    task_descriptions = Counter()\\n\\n    with open(args.input) as f:\\n        for line_no, line in enumerate(f, 1):\\n            line = rejected.append((line_no, f\\\"json_error: {e}\\\"))\\n                reasons[\\\"json_error\\\"] += 1\\n                continue\\n            ok, reason, n_turns, actions = validate_example(\\n fix_answer(m):\\\\n        old = m.group(1)\\\\n        new = fix_move_action(old)\\\\n        if old != new:\\\\n            stats[\\\\\\\"answer_fixed\\\\\\\"] += 1\\\\n        return f\\\\\\\"&lt;answer>{new}&lt;/answer>\\\\\\\"\\\\n    \\\\n    content = re.sub(r'&lt;answer>(.*?)&lt;/answer>', fix_answer, content, flags=re.DOTALL)\\\\n    \\\\n    # Fix &lt;think> content (Suggestion field)\\\\n    def fix_think(m):\\\\n        old = m.group(1)\\\\n        new = fix_move_action(old)\\\\n        if old != new:\\\\n            stats[\\\\\\\"think_fixed\\\\\\\"] += 1\\\\n        return f\\\\\\\"&lt;think>{new}&lt;/think>\\\\\\\"\\\\n    \\\\n    content = re.sub(r'&lt;think>(.*?)&lt;/think>', fix_think, content, flags=re.DOTALL)\\\\n    \\\\n    return content, stats\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef validate_format(content: str) -> dict:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Validate a single assistant message matches RAGEN format.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    has_think = bool(re.search(r'&lt;think>.*?&lt;/think>', content, re.DOTALL))\\\\n    has_answer = bool(re.search(r'&lt;answer>.*?&lt;/answer>', content, re.DOTALL))\\\\n    full_match = bool(re.search(r'&lt;think>.*?&lt;/think>\\\\\\\\s*&lt;answer>.*?&lt;/answer>', content, re.DOTALL))\\\\n    \\\\n    action = \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    m = re.search(r'&lt;answer>(.*?)&lt;/answer>', content, re.DOTALL)\\\\n    if m:\\\\n        action = m.group(1).strip()\\\\n    \\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"has_think\\\\\\\": has_think,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"has_answer\\\\\\\": has_answer,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"full_match\\\\\\\": full_match,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"action\\\\\\\": action,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"has_move\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"move \\\\\\\" in action and len(entries),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"total_assistant_msgs\\\\\\\": 0,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"answer_fixed\\\\\\\": 0,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"think_fixed\\\\\\\": 0,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"pre_format_ok\\\\\\\": I'm checking if the replay succeeded, and if there were any errors during the environment steps, I'll skip this entry and increment the error counter. Otherwise, I'm augmenting the user messages by injecting the admissible commands at each step, then storing the updated messages in the augmented dataset and tracking the count of successfully augmented entries.\n\nNow I'm cleaning up the lookup table by removing the matched observation, printing progress every 500 games to show how many have been augmented and how many remain unmatched, then closing the environment and writing the final output file—either the augmented version if available, or the original entry.\n\nBut I'm realizing there are some real problems with this matching strategy. json.load(f)['completed']\ngf = [g for g in game_files if os.path.exists(g)][0]\nprint('Game:', gf)\n\nrequest_infos = textworld.EnvInfos(won=True, admissible_commands=True)\ndef make_demangler(env):\n    return AlfredDemangler(env, shuffle=False)\n\nenv = textworld.start(gf, request_infos=request_infos, wrappers=[make_demangler, AlfredInfos])\nstate = env.reset()\nprint('Type:', type(state))\nattrs = [a for a in dir(state) if 'admissible' in a.lower() or 'command' in a.lower()]\nprint('Relevant attrs:', attrs)\nif hasattr(state, {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01F2okNiH5ENG6je58i3TqJR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PWaMEEbRdxptNSbPM76pxD","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"bjb1-17606","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/cost-verify-agent-rl && python3 -c \"\nimport os, json, re\nos.environ['ALFWORLD_DATA'] = '/root/autodl-tmp/alfworld'\nimport textworld\nfrom alfworld.agents.environment.alfred_tw_env import AlfredDemangler, AlfredInfos\n\n# Load a pick_and_place game and check if 'move' appears in admissible\nwith open('/root/autodl-tmp/expert_trajectories/progress.json') as f:\n    game_files = json.load(f)['completed']\ngf_list = [g for g in game_files if 'pick_and_place_simple' in g and os.path.exists(g)][:3]\n\nrequest_infos = textworld.EnvInfos(won=True, admissible_commands=True)\ndef make_demangler(env):\n    return AlfredDemangler(env, shuffle=False)\nenv = textworld.start(gf_list[0], request_infos=request_infos, wrappers=[make_demangler, AlfredInfos])\n\n# Load a pick_and_place game, take an object, then check admissible\nfor gf in gf_list:\n    state = env.load(gf)\n    state = env.reset()\n    # Find 'take' action\n    take_actions = [a for a in state.admissible_commands if a.startswith('take') or a.startswith('go')]\n    # Play a few random steps to see if move appears\n    for step in range(30):\n        # Check for move/put in admissible\n        move_cmds = [a for a in state.admissible_commands if a.startswith('move') or a.startswith('put')]\n        if move_cmds:\n            print(f'Game: {os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(gf)))}')\n            print(f'  Step {step}: move/put cmds = {move_cmds}')\n            break\n        # Take first admissible action that seems useful\n        for a in state.admissible_commands:\n            if a.startswith('take') or a.startswith('go to') or a.startswith('open'):\n                state, _, done = env.step(a)\n                break\n        else:\n            state, _, done = env.step(state.admissible_commands[0])\n        if done:\n            break\n\nenv.close()\n\"\n","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":12334,"cache_read_input_tokens":53420,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":12334},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"76290795-5d01-45f7-ac99-5018aad351eb","uuid":"807c0613-48b5-44d6-aa39-0a2859c11439"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01F8g1c9XdJzYhfKZykDS5wy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Wvj7udSX4Q61mMGw4YKJQX","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"bjb1-17606","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/cost-verify-agent-rl && python3 -c \"\nimport os, json\nos.environ['ALFWORLD_DATA'] = '/root/autodl-tmp/alfworld'\nimport textworld\nfrom alfworld.agents.environment.alfred_tw_env import AlfredDemangler, AlfredInfos\n\nwith open('/root/autodl-tmp/expert_trajectories/progress.json') as f:\n    game_files = json.load(f)['completed']\n# Pick a pick_and_place game\ngf = [g for g in game_files if 'pick_and_place_simple-Potato-None-Microwave' in g and os.path.exists(g)][0]\nprint('Game:', gf)\n\nrequest_infos = textworld.EnvInfos(won=True, admissible_commands=True)\ndef make_demangler(env):\n    return AlfredDemangler(env, shuffle=False)\nenv = textworld.start(gf, request_infos=request_infos, wrappers=[make_demangler, AlfredInfos])\n\nstate = env.reset()\nobs = state.feedback\nif '-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-' in obs:\n    obs = obs.split('-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-')[-1].strip()\nprint('Init obs:', obs[:300])\nprint('Admissible:', state.admissible_commands[:10])\nprint()\n\n# Play through: look for potato, pick it up, go to target\nfor step in range(20):\n    # Find useful action\n    ac = state.admissible_commands\n    chosen = None\n    for a in ac:\n        if 'take potato' in a:\n            chosen = a\n            break\n    if chosen is None:\n        for a in ac:\n            if 'go to' in a and ('counter' in a or 'fridge' in a or 'micro' in a or 'cabinet' in a or 'sink' in a):\n                chosen = a\n                break\n    if chosen is None:\n        for a in ac:\n            if a.startswith('open') or a.startswith('go to'):\n                chosen = a\n                break\n    if chosen is None:\n        chosen = ac[0]\n    \n    state, reward, done = env.step(chosen)\n    obs = state.feedback\n    print(f'Step {step}: action={chosen}')\n    print(f'  obs: {obs[:150]}')\n    # Show all admissible commands (look for move/put)\n    move_put = [a for a in state.admissible_commands if 'move' in a or 'put' in a]\n    if move_put:\n        print(f'  *** MOVE/PUT found: {move_put}')\n    print(f'  admissible count: {len(state.admissible_commands)}')\n    if done:\n        print('  DONE')\n        break\n\nenv.close()\n\"\n","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":970,"cache_read_input_tokens":65754,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":970},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"76290795-5d01-45f7-ac99-5018aad351eb","uuid":"a1a737ec-ec20-439d-953e-158177b415db"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01RxLc6G6QNrKHNzM5g1ETED","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BdEhrZeqKADK5AFQYUhhSX","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"bjb1-17606","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/cost-verify-agent-rl && python3 -c \"\nimport os, json, re\nos.environ['ALFWORLD_DATA'] = '/root/autodl-tmp/alfworld'\nimport textworld\nfrom alfworld.agents.environment.alfred_tw_env import AlfredDemangler, AlfredInfos\n\n# Read one SFT entry that has 'move' action\nwith open('/root/autodl-tmp/expert_sft/expert_sft_train.jsonl') as f:\n    for line in f:\n        d = json.loads(line)\n        actions = []\n        for m in d['messages']:\n            if m['role'] == 'assistant':\n                am = re.search(r'&lt;answer>(.*?)&lt;/answer>', m['content'])\n                if am:\n                    actions.append(am.group(1).strip())\n        if any(a.startswith('move') for a in actions):\n            print('Found entry with move action')\n            first_obs = None\n            for m in d['messages']:\n                if m['role'] == 'user':\n                    first_obs = m['content']\n                    break\n            print('First obs[:200]:', first_obs[:200])\n            print('Actions:', actions)\n            print()\n            \n            # Now find matching game file and replay\n            with open('/root/autodl-tmp/expert_trajectories/progress.json') as pf:\n                game_files = json.load(pf)['completed']\n            \n            # Try to match by initial observation\n            request_infos = textworld.EnvInfos(won=True, admissible_commands=True)\n            def make_demangler(env):\n                return AlfredDemangler(env, shuffle=False)\n            \n            gf0 = [g for g in game_files if os.path.exists(g)][0]\n            env = textworld.start(gf0, request_infos=request_infos, wrappers=[make_demangler, AlfredInfos])\n            \n            # Search for matching game\n            obs_target = first_obs[:100]  # first 100 chars for matching\n            found = False\n            for gi, gf in enumerate(game_files):\n                if not os.path.exists(gf):\n                    continue\n                try:\n                    state = env.load(gf)\n                    state = env.reset()\n                    obs = state.feedback\n                    if '-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-' in obs:\n                        obs = obs.split('-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-')[-1].strip()\n                    if obs[:100] == obs_target:\n                        print(f'MATCH at game {gi}: {gf}')\n                        # Replay with expert actions\n                        print(f'Initial admissible ({len(state.admissible_commands)}): {state.admissible_commands[:5]}...')\n                        for ai, action in enumerate(actions):\n                            state, reward, done = env.step(action)\n                            move_put = [a for a in state.admissible_commands if 'move' in a or 'put' in a]\n                            print(f'Step {ai}: action={action}, obs={state.feedback[:80]}, move_put={move_put}')\n                            if done:\n                                print('DONE, won:', state.has_won if hasattr(state, 'has_won') else 'unknown')\n                                break\n                        found = True\n                        break\n                except Exception as e:\n                    continue\n                if gi > 500 and not found:\n                    print('Searched 500 games, no match')\n                    break\n            \n            env.close()\n            break\n\"\n","account":"root","warn":true},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2247,"cache_read_input_tokens":66724,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2247},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"76290795-5d01-45f7-ac99-5018aad351eb","uuid":"b5a996b4-48a8-4b7a-952f-4520b076e433"}

Full observation matching\n            norm_target = ' '.join(first_obs.split())\n            found = False\n            for gi, gf in enumerate(game_files):\n                if not os.path.exists(gf):\n                    continue\n                try:\n                    state = env.load(gf)\n                    state = env.reset()\n                    obs = state.feedback\n                    if '-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-' in obs:\n                        obs = obs.split('-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-')[-1].strip()\n                    norm_obs = ' '.join(obs.split())\n                    if norm_obs == norm_target:\n                        print(f'EXACT MATCH at game {gi}: {os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(gf)))}')\n                        # Replay\n                        for ai, action in enumerate(actions):\n                            ac_before = state.admissible_commands\n                            move_put_before = [a for a in ac_before if 'move' in a or 'put' in a]\n                            if move_put_before:\n                                print(f'  Step {ai}: BEFORE step, move/put in admissible: {move_put_before}')\n                            state, reward, done = env.step(action)\n                            print(f'  Step {ai}: action={action}, feedback={state.feedback[:80]}')\n                            if done:\n                                won = getattr(state, 'has_won', None)\n                                print(f'  DONE, has_won={won}')\n                                break\n                        found = True\n                        break\n                except:\n                    continue\n            \n            if not found:\n                print(f'No match found after scanning all {len(game_files)} games')\n            env.close()\n            break\n\" 2>&1 | head -30\n","account":"root","warn":true},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1918,"cache_read_input_tokens":68971,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1918},"output_tokens":27,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"76290795-5d01-45f7-ac99-5018aad351eb","uuid":"0297804b-e01d-4629-9748-1f37dcd1d970"}

\\\"def trajectory_to_sft(trajectory):\\\\n    messages = [{\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"system\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": SYSTEM_PROMPT}]\\\\n    init_obs = clean_obs(trajectory[0][\\\\\\\"obs\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    messages.append({\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"user\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": init_obs})\\\\n\\\\n    for i in range(1, len(trajectory)):\\\\n        entry = trajectory[i]\\\\n        action = entry[\\\\\\\"action\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        obs = clean_obs(entry[\\\\\\\"obs\\\\\\\"])\\\\n        is_last = (i == len(trajectory) - 1)\\\\n        prev_obs = clean_obs(trajectory[i - 1][\\\\\\\"obs\\\\\\\"])\\\\n\\\\n        if is_last:\\\\n            assessment, reasoning = \\\\\\\"positive\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"Task completed successfully.\\\\\\\"\\\\n        else:\\\\n            assessment, reasoning = infer_assessment(prev_obs, action)\\\\n\\\\n        assistant_content = (\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"&lt;think>[Assessment: {assessment}] \\\\\\\"\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"[Reasoning: {reasoning}] \\\\\\\"\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"[Suggestion: {action}]&lt;/think>\\\\\\\"\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"&lt;answer>{action}&lt;/answer>\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n        messages.append({\\\\\\\"role\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"assistant\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"content\\\\\\\": assistant_content})\\\\n\\\\n Need a different approach.\\\"\\n    if \\\"you open\\\" in p:\\n        return \\\"neutral\\\", \\\"Opened the receptacle to check contents.\\\"\\n    if \\\"you clean\\\" in p:\\n        return \\\"positive\\\", \\\"Successfully cleaned the object.\\\"\\n    if \\\"you heat\\\" in p:\\n        return \\\"positive\\\", \\\"Successfully heated the object.\\\"\\n    if \\\"you cool\\\" in p:\\n        return \\\"positive\\\", \\\"Successfully cooled the object.\\\"\\n    if \\\"you use\\\" in p:\\n        return \\\"positive\\\", \\\"Used the lamp to examine the object.\\\"\\n    if \\\"closed\\\" in p:\\n        return \\\"neutral\\\", \\\"Found a closed receptacle. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Ku6jmiRJGdoGTcR3vfZBcA","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Amz5NsReMQCRsdbqP8Qrcd","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"bjb1-17606","command":"cat > /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/scripts/augment_admissible_v2.py << 'PYEOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Re-collect expert trajectories WITH admissible actions for exp019b.\n\nRe-runs HandCodedTWAgent on all train games, recording admissible_commands\nat each step. Outputs SFT data with admissible actions in RAGEN format:\n  \"{obs}\\n\\nAdmissible actions: [{action1}, {action2}, ...]\"\n\nOutput: /root/autodl-tmp/expert_sft_v3/expert_sft_{train,val}.jsonl\n\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nimport json\nimport random\nimport re\nimport argparse\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nos.environ[\"ALFWORLD_DATA\"] = \"/root/autodl-tmp/alfworld\"\n\nimport textworld\nfrom alfworld.agents.expert import HandCodedTWAgent, HandCodedAgentTimeout, HandCodedAgentFailed\nfrom alfworld.agents.environment.alfred_tw_env import AlfredDemangler, AlfredInfos\n\n\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\n    \"You're a helpful assistant. You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\n\"\n    \"Complete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Before each action, you MUST first produce a self-guidance assessment:\\n\"\n    \"[Assessment: positive/neutral/negative] - evaluate your current progress.\\n\"\n    \"[Reasoning: one sentence analyzing what has been accomplished and what remains.]\\n\"\n    \"[Suggestion: the best next action from admissible actions.]\\n\\n\"\n    \"Your reasoning with self-guidance MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\n\"\n    \"Then choose an admissible action and present it within &lt;answer>...&lt;/answer> tags.\"\n)\n\nSUPPORTED_TASK_TYPES = {\n    \"pick_and_place_simple\",\n    \"look_at_obj_in_light\",\n    \"pick_clean_then_place_in_recep\",\n    \"pick_heat_then_place_in_recep\",\n    \"pick_cool_then_place_in_recep\",\n    \"pick_two_obj_and_place\",\n}\n\nALL_KNOWN_TASK_TYPES = SUPPORTED_TASK_TYPES | {\"pick_and_place_with_movable_recep\"}\n\nANSWER_RE = re.compile(r\"&lt;answer>(.*?)&lt;/answer>\", re.DOTALL)\nTHINK_RE = re.compile(r\"&lt;think>.*?&lt;/think>\", re.DOTALL)\n\nVALID_ACTION_PREFIXES = (\n    \"go to \", \"open \", \"close \", \"take \", \"put \", \"use \",\n    \"look\", \"inventory\", \"examine \", \"clean \", \"heat \", \"cool \",\n    \"move \", \"slice \",\n)\n\n\ndef find_game_files(data_dir, split=\"train\"):\n    split_dir = os.path.join(data_dir, split)\n    game_files = []\n    skipped = Counter()\n    for task_dir in sorted(os.listdir(split_dir)):\n        task_path = os.path.join(split_dir, task_dir)\n        if not os.path.isdir(task_path):\n            continue\n        task_type = None\n        for tt in ALL_KNOWN_TASK_TYPES:\n            if task_dir.startswith(tt + \"-\"):\n                task_type = tt\n                break\n        if task_type is None or task_type not in SUPPORTED_TASK_TYPES:\n            skipped[task_type or \"unknown\"] += 1\n            continue\n        for trial_dir in sorted(os.listdir(task_path)):\n            trial_path = os.path.join(task_path, trial_dir)\n            if not os.path.isdir(trial_path):\n                continue\n            game_file = os.path.join(trial_path, \"game.tw-pddl\")\n            traj_file = os.path.join(trial_path, \"traj_data.json\")\n            if os.path.exists(game_file) and os.path.exists(traj_file):\n                game_files.append(game_file)\n    if skipped:\n        print(f\"Skipped task types: {dict(skipped)}\")\n    return game_files\n\n\ndef clean_obs(obs):\n    if \"-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-\" in obs:\n        obs = obs.split(\"-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-\")[-1]\n    return obs.strip()\n\n\ndef format_observation(obs, admissible_actions):\n    actions_str = \", \".join(admissible_actions)\n    return f\"{obs}\\n\\nAdmissible actions: [{actions_str}]\"\n\n\ndef infer_assessment(prev_obs, action):\n    p = prev_obs.lower()\n    a = (action or \"\").lower()\n    if \"you pick up\" in p or \"you take\" in p:\n        return \"positive\", \"Successfully picked up the object.\"\n    if \"you put\" in p or \"you move\" in p:\n        return \"positive\", \"Successfully placed the object.\"\n    if \"nothing happens\" in p:\n        return \"negative\", \"Action had no effect. Need a different approach.\"\n    if \"you open\" in p:\n        return \"neutral\", \"Opened the receptacle to check contents.\"\n    if \"you clean\" in p:\n        return \"positive\", \"Successfully cleaned the object.\"\n    if \"you heat\" in p:\n        return \"positive\", \"Successfully heated the object.\"\n    if \"you cool\" in p:\n        return \"positive\", \"Successfully cooled the object.\"\n    if \"you use\" in p:\n        return \"positive\", \"Used the lamp to examine the object.\"\n    if \"closed\" in p:\n        return \"neutral\", \"Found a closed receptacle. Should open it.\"\n    if \"you see nothing\" in p:\n        return \"negative\", \"Nothing useful here. Searching elsewhere.\"\n    if \"you see\" in p:\n        return \"neutral\", \"Observing the area for the target object.\"\n    if \"go to\" in a:\n        return \"neutral\", \"Moving to check another location.\"\n    if \"look\" in a:\n        return \"neutral\", \"Looking around to assess the situation.\"\n    return \"neutral\", \"Continuing to work on the task.\"\n\n\ndef trajectory_to_sft_with_admissible(trajectory):\n    messages = [{\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": SYSTEM_PROMPT}]\n    init_obs = clean_obs(trajectory[0][\"obs\"])\n    init_admissible = trajectory[0].get(\"admissible\", [])\n    messages.append({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": format_observation(init_obs, init_admissible)})\n\n    for i in range(1, len(trajectory)):\n        entry = trajectory[i]\n        action = entry[\"action\"]\n        obs = clean_obs(entry[\"obs\"])\n        admissible = entry.get(\"admissible\", [])\n        is_last = (i == len(trajectory) - 1)\n        prev_obs = clean_obs(trajectory[i - 1][\"obs\"])\n\n        if is_last:\n            assessment, reasoning = \"positive\", \"Task completed successfully.\"\n        else:\n            assessment, reasoning = infer_assessment(prev_obs, action)\n\n        assistant_content = (\n            f\"&lt;think>[Assessment: {assessment}] \"\n            f\"[Reasoning: {reasoning}] \"\n            f\"[Suggestion: {action}]&lt;/think>\"\n            f\"&lt;answer>{action}&lt;/answer>\"\n        )\n        messages.append({\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": assistant_content})\n        if not is_last:\n            messages.append({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": format_observation(obs, admissible)})\n\n    return {\"messages\": messages}\n\n\ndef collect_single_game(env, agent, game_file, max_steps=150):\n    try:\n        env.load(game_file)\n        state = env.reset()\n    except Exception as e:\n        return None, f\"load_error: {e}\"\n\n    try:\n        agent.reset(game=game_file)\n        agent.observe(state.feedback)\n    except Exception as e:\n        return None, f\"agent_init_error: {e}\"\n\n    done = False\n    prev_action = \"\"\n    initial_admissible = list(state.admissible_commands) if hasattr(state, 'admissible_commands') else []\n    trajectory = [{\"obs\": state.feedback, \"action\": None, \"admissible\": initial_admissible}]\n\n    for step in range(max_steps):\n        try:\n            action = agent.act(state, 0, done, prev_action)\n        except (HandCodedAgentTimeout, HandCodedAgentFailed):\n            return None, \"agent_failed\"\n        except Exception as e:\n            return None, f\"act_error: {e}\"\n\n        try:\n            state, reward, done = env.step(action)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return None, f\"step_error: {e}\"\n\n        prev_action = action\n        step_admissible = list(state.admissible_commands) if hasattr(state, 'admissible_commands') else []\n        trajectory.append({\"obs\": state.feedback, \"action\": action, \"admissible\": step_admissible})\n\n        if done:\n            break\n\n    won = state.get(\"won\", False) if hasattr(state, \"get\") else getattr(state, \"won\", False)\n    if won:\n        return trajectory, \"ok\"\n    return None, \"not_won\"\n\n\ndef is_valid_action(action):\n    a = action.strip().lower()\n    return any(a.startswith(p) or a == p.strip() for p in VALID_ACTION_PREFIXES)\n\n\ndef validate_example(ex, min_turns=2, max_turns=40):\n    if not isinstance(ex, dict) or \"messages\" not in ex:\n        return False, \"no_messages\"\n    msgs = ex[\"messages\"]\n    if not msgs or msgs[0][\"role\"] != \"system\":\n        return False, \"no_system\"\n    if len(msgs) < 3 or msgs[1][\"role\"] != \"user\":\n        return False, \"no_user_task\"\n\n    assistant_turns = 0\n    for m in msgs[2:]:\n        role = m.get(\"role\")\n        content = m.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        if role == \"assistant\":\n            assistant_turns += 1\n            ans = ANSWER_RE.search(content)\n            if not ans:\n                return False, \"missing_answer_tag\"\n            if not THINK_RE.search(content):\n                return False, \"missing_think_tag\"\n            action = ans.group(1).strip()\n            if not is_valid_action(action):\n                return False, \"invalid_action\"\n        elif role == \"user\":\n            continue\n        else:\n            return False, f\"bad_role:{role}\"\n\n    if assistant_turns < min_turns:\n        return False, \"too_short\"\n    if assistant_turns > max_turns:\n        return False, \"too_long\"\n    return True, \"ok\"\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--data-dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/alfworld/json_2.1.1\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output-dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/expert_sft_v3\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--max-games\", type=int, default=0, help=\"0 = all\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--max-steps\", type=int, default=150)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--split\", default=\"train\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--val-frac\", type=float, default=0.05)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    game_files = find_game_files(args.data_dir, args.split)\n    print(f\"Found {len(game_files)} game files in {args.split} split\")\n\n    if args.max_games > 0:\n        game_files = game_files[:args.max_games]\n        print(f\"Limited to {args.max_games} games\")\n\n    request_infos = textworld.EnvInfos(won=True, admissible_commands=True, facts=True)\n\n    def make_demangler(env):\n        return AlfredDemangler(env, shuffle=False)\n\n    env = textworld.start(\n        game_files[0],\n        request_infos=request_infos,\n        wrappers=[make_demangler, AlfredInfos],\n    )\n    agent = HandCodedTWAgent(max_steps=args.max_steps)\n\n    all_sft = []\n    stats = Counter()\n    move_action_count = 0\n    put_action_count = 0\n    admissible_move_count = 0\n    admissible_put_count = 0\n\n    for idx, game_file in enumerate(game_files):\n        trajectory, status = collect_single_game(env, agent, game_file, args.max_steps)\n\n        if status == \"ok\" and trajectory:\n            sft_data = trajectory_to_sft_with_admissible(trajectory)\n            ok, reason = validate_example(sft_data)\n            if ok:\n                all_sft.append(sft_data)\n                for step in trajectory:\n                    if step[\"action\"]:\n                        if step[\"action\"].startswith(\"move \"):\n                            move_action_count += 1\n                        if step[\"action\"].startswith(\"put \"):\n                            put_action_count += 1\n                    for ac in step.get(\"admissible\", []):\n                        if ac.startswith(\"move \"):\n                            admissible_move_count += 1\n                        if ac.startswith(\"put \"):\n                            admissible_put_count += 1\n            else:\n                stats[f\"validate_{reason}\"] += 1\n\n        stats[status] += 1\n\n        if (idx + 1) % 500 == 0 or idx == len(game_files) - 1:\n            print(\n                f\"[{idx + 1}/{len(game_files)}] \"\n                f\"valid_sft={len(all_sft)} | stats={dict(stats)} | \"\n                f\"move_actions={move_action_count} put_actions={put_action_count} | \"\n                f\"admissible_move={admissible_move_count} admissible_put={admissible_put_count}\"\n            )\n\n    env.close()\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal valid SFT examples: {len(all_sft)}\")\n\n    if not all_sft:\n        print(\"ERROR: No valid examples collected!\")\n        return\n\n    random.Random(args.seed).shuffle(all_sft)\n    n_val = max(1, int(len(all_sft) * args.val_frac))\n    val, train = all_sft[:n_val], all_sft[n_val:]\n\n    train_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"expert_sft_train.jsonl\")\n    with open(train_path, \"w\") as f:\n        for ex in train:\n            f.write(json.dumps(ex, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n    print(f\"Wrote train: {train_path} ({len(train)} examples)\")\n\n    val_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \"expert_sft_val.jsonl\")\n    with open(val_path, \"w\") as f:\n        for ex in val:\n            f.write(json.dumps(ex, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n    print(f\"Wrote val: {val_path} ({len(val)} examples)\")\n\n    # Sample output\n    print(\"\\n=== Sample entry (first user message) ===\")\n    sample = train[0]\n    for m in sample[\"messages\"][:3]:\n        content = m[\"content\"]\n        if len(content) > 300:\n            content = content[:300] + \"...\"\n        print(f\"[{m['role']}] {content}\")\n    print()\n\n    # Check admissible actions in output\n    admissible_found = 0\n    move_preserved = 0\n    for ex in train[:50]:\n        for m in ex[\"messages\"]:\n            if m[\"role\"] == \"user\" and \"Admissible actions:\" in m[\"content\"]:\n                admissible_found += 1\n                break\n        for m in ex[\"messages\"]:\n            if m[\"role\"] == \"assistant\" and \"move \" in m[\"content\"]:\n                move_preserved += 1\n                break\n    print(f\"Verification (first 50 train): admissible_found={admissible_found}/50, move_preserved={move_preserved}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF\necho \"Script written
